## Document 002: 10月CPI再超预期，基本锁定12月美联储加息，升息幅度仍有不确定性

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月CPI再超预期，基本锁定12月美联储加息，升息幅度仍有不确定性.pdf
Report source: 浦银国际证券
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

从环比细分数据来看，10 月核心商品和住房 CPI 通胀率均略微下滑，
但运输、 医疗等服务价格仍有韧性， 核心通胀降温仍不顺畅（图表 1）。

 核心商品价格环比通胀率回落 0.09 个百分点到 0.16%， 二手车价
格转跌形成明显拖累， 高油价传导效应开始显现。其中，运输 CPI
环比通胀率部分因为二手车价格大跌而走低 0.77 个百分点到 -
0.38%。教育和通讯商品 CPI 环比通胀率亦收窄 0.37 个百分点到
0.5%，或受到计算机、外围设备 CPI 环比通胀率下滑的影响。这
或暗示着芯片价格暴涨引起的相关产业链价格大涨的最坏时期
已经过去。与此同时，8-9 月连续低迷的家具和医疗护理商品价格
10 月环比增速显著改善。家具价格的上升或与油价传导有关。娱
乐商品 CPI 环比通胀率略微下滑，但仍维持 0.71%的较高水平。
二手车涨价趋势的逆转表明，前期部分推升因素确有波动性，但
其他商品价格回升抵消了部分降温作用。核心商品尚未回到持续
通缩状态。

 住房 CPI 环比通胀率 10 月仅略微回落 0.09 个百分点到 0.3%，仍
是通胀重要来源。尽管涨幅有所收窄，住房仍贡献了超过三成的
当月核心 CPI 增幅， 反映高权重分项的通胀粘性仍强。其中，最为
关键的业主等价租金 CPI 环比通胀率 10 月仅小幅下滑 0.03 个百
分点到 0.32%。主要居所租金 CPI 环比通胀率微降至 0.2%。但考虑
到房价趋势，我们依然认为该分项有下降空间，后续需观察租金
和业主等价租金 CPI 环比通胀率能否连续放缓。外宿 CPI 环比通
胀率从 9 月的 2.04%下滑到 0.64%。

 排除了住房的核心服务 CPI（超级核心服务 CPI）环比通胀率 10
月反弹 0.05 个百分点到 0.39%，压力仍较为突出。娱乐和运输服
务 CPI 环比通胀率的上升是主要推动力， 两者均在10 月升到 0.8%
以上的较高水平。细看运输服务分项，油价涨幅的收窄推动机票
价格环比增速下滑（10 月：0.4%，9 月：0.4%）， 但汽车保险价格
浦银国际 
数据点评 
10月CPI再超预期，基本锁定12月美联储加息，升息幅度仍有不确定性

### Page 2

10 月美国非农新增就业人数明显超预期， 有助于减轻美联储对劳动力
市场走弱的顾虑。10 月非农新增就业 16.5 万人， 远高于路透调查预期
的 6.5 万人，主要由专业和商业服务、医疗保健及运输仓储业拉动。8
月和 9 月新增就业分别上修至 18.6 万和 6.0 万人，合计上修 8.4 万人
（图表 2） 。在劳动参与率小幅上升的情况下（上升 0.1 个百分点到
61.9%） ， 失业率由9 月的 4.174%略微下降至 4.141%， 显示就业人数增
长快于劳动力人口 （图表 3） 。 不过， 因经济原因被迫兼职的人数增加，
广义失业率（U-6）由 7.6%升至 7.9%。与修订后的 9 月数据比较，私
营部门平均时薪环比增速由 0.3%加快至 0.4%，同比增速则由 3.2%微
降至 3.1%，工资趋势仍较温和。就业超预期减轻了美联储对劳动力市
场走弱的顾虑，但非农新增就业数据今年以来波动较大，我们认为并
不需要对单一数据过度解读。接下来，若财政刺激的边际提振减弱、
经济动能放缓， 叠加AI 应用压低部分岗位的招聘需求， 我们认为就业
增长或仍逐步降温。

10 月继续超预期的核心 CPI 数据再次凸显核心通胀粘性，高油价传导
效应开始显现。二手车价格已经回落、住房价格涨幅亦放缓，但核心
CPI 连续第三个月环比上涨 0.3%，说明基础价格压力尚未明显消退。
此外，此前仅主要反映在机票 CPI 的高油价传导效应 10 月开始向家
具、城际交通 CPI 等分项传导。不过， 各分项走势仍有分化， 单月数据
尚不足以确认全面再通胀。

接下来，油价回落有望缓和后续整体通胀，但核心通胀回落仍可能偏
慢。特朗普 11 月 9 日表示暂缓进一步对伊朗军事打击， 但仍维持海上
封锁，美伊尚未达成最终协议。布伦特原油期货 11 月 10 日盘中一度
跌破每桶 90 美元，较 10 月底高点明显回落。若局势继续缓和、油价
维持较低水平，汽油等能源分项对通胀的推升作用有望进一步减弱 。
然而，美联储 11 月 9 日发布的研究显示， 疫情后油价向核心通胀的传
导有所增强，前期高能源成本仍可能滞后影响商品与服务定价 ，这一
点在 10 月数据中已有所体现。 与此同时，AI 数据中心扩建可能加剧局
部电力和施工资源约束，尽管民主党赢得国会控制权以及部分关键州
州长竞选或对数据中心的建设形成一定阻力。不过，9 月实际可支配
收入环比下降 0.3%、实际消费近乎持平，家庭购买力承压或限制企业
向消费者转嫁成本的空间。

### Page 3

我们维持 12 月美联储加息 25 个基点的基准判断，但单次加息 50 个
基点的风险正在上升。10 月美联储会议表明通胀仍高、且为追加紧缩
保留依据，但沃什对政策已开始见效的评价增加了观望空间。 从基本
面来看，10 月就业超预期及此前月份数据上修缓解了美联储对劳动力
市场走弱的顾虑，核心 CPI 连续三个月上涨 0.3%则强化了追加紧缩的
理由。美联储理事沃勒 11 月 10 日在美国 CPI 数据公布前表示，若核
心 CPI 环比仍在 0.3%或更高，应考虑 12 月加息 50 个基点。本次核心
CPI 读数达到其提出的条件， 但这一表态仍属沃勒个人观点， 不能等同
于 FOMC 共识。 考虑到近期油价明显回落、 住房和核心商品涨幅放缓，
我们仍维持 12 月美联储再加息 25 个基点的基本判断，但由于超预期
的 10 月通胀和就业数据，12 月单次加息 50 个基点的风险也明显上
升，后续需关注其他官员是否跟进支持， 以及11 月非农就业数据、10
月 PCE 通胀数据发布和通胀预期变化。11 月 CPI 通胀数据发布时间将
晚于 12 月 8-9 日召开的 12 月议息会议。对于明年政策路径，需重点
关注 12 月经济预测及利率预测的调整，以及潜在的 5 个工作小组的报
告，后者可能也会影响美联储对利率路径的判断。目前来看，我们仍
倾向于认为美联储本轮是预防性加息，而非过热性加息。

在中期选举的影响问题上，我们认为国会控制权易手对美联储短期政
策的影响或有限。民主党控制两院减少了一项潜在的额外需求刺激，这
有助于降低进一步加息的尾部风险。此外， 当前更为制衡的美国政治环
境亦有可能放缓美国 AI 投资发展， 从而缓解从AI 投资扩张带来的价格
压力。不过，具体的货币政策走势仍依赖于通胀和就业之间的平衡。

另外，联邦法官、内阁及高级官员任命由参议院确认。在民主党控制
参议院的情况下，美联储理事的任命或受阻（地区联储行长不经参议
院确认） 。 未来任何美联储理事会的提名都有可能遭到冻结。 然而， 目
前七名理事席位均已满，沃什也已就任主席，短期内并不会自然形成
理事会席位长期空缺。 只有在2027 年后席位出现空缺， 民主党掌控下
的参议院才可通过延迟确认来限制特朗普继续调整理事会构成（参见：
民主党在美国中期选举中大获全胜，将如何影响美国经济和金融市
场？）。

风险提示： 通胀上行风险包括美伊局势反复推升油价， 前期能源成本
向核心商品和服务价格的传导强于预期， 以及AI 投资加剧电力、 原材
料和施工资源供需紧张，导致核心通胀粘性超预期，美联储加息幅度
或后续紧缩力度超过基准判断。下行风险包括美伊局势持续缓和、油
价回落快于预期，住房通胀降温加快，以及就业、消费或AI 投资走弱
超预期，促使通胀更快回落并降低进一步加息的必要性。

### Page 6

## Document 022: 158円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 ～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の“三つの分岐点”が方向性を決める～

Source PDF: all report pdf/158円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 ～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の“三つの分岐点”が方向性を決める～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Investment Navigator

2026 年 10 月 22 日

1/6 
158 円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 
～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の"三つの分岐点"が方向性を決める～ 
 経済調査部 シニア・フェロー  嶌峰 義清 
（℡：050-5474-7489）

（要旨） 
○ ドル円相場は、7 月末の日米協調介入、9 月上旬の急激な円高、9 月の日米同時利上げを経て、
10 月に入ってからは1 ドル＝158 円前後の狭いレンジでの推移が続いている。円先物市場に
おける非商業部門の建玉は、9 月前半に過去に例のない規模で円の売り越しから買い越しへ
と転じた。しかし、その後は買い越し幅が縮小し、売建玉が再び増え始めている。投機筋の
円買い意欲は弱まりつつあると言えよう。 
○ 期待インフレ率 （BEI） で実質化した日米の実質金利差は、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債のいず
れでも 2％ポイントを超える米国優位が続いている。名目金利差は縮小したものの、米国の
金利上昇は実質金利の上昇、日本のは期待インフレ率の上昇によるところが大きい。このた
め、実質ベースのドル優位は崩れていない。 
○ 当面の焦点は、①11 月 3 日の米中間選挙、②選挙後のイラン情勢、③日銀の 12 月利上げと
その後のペース、の三つである。民主党が上下両院を制し、イラン情勢が沈静化に向かい、
日銀が利上げ路線を明確にすれば、円高方向への転換が視野に入る。逆の組み合わせとなれ
ば、再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が強まる展開も否定できない。

1. 投機筋の円ポジションは"円売り一色"から変化し、158 円を挟んで膠着が続くドル円相場 
ドル円相場は、方向感を欠いた推移が続いている。10 月入り後の値動きは、高値が158.94 円
（10 月14 日） 、安値が156.99 円（10 月2 日）と、月間の値幅は2 円弱にとどまる。 
もっとも、ここに至るまでの道のりは平坦ではなかった。4 月末から5 月の大型連休中には、
160 円台後半まで進んだ円安に対して、政府・日銀が総額11.7 兆円の円買い介入を実施した。そ
れでも円安基調は止まらず、7 月 28 日には163.91 円（ECB参照値）と、約40 年ぶりの円安水準を
つけた。これに対し、7 月 31 日には日米当局が28 年ぶりとなる円買い協調介入に踏み切った。介
入額は 15 兆3993 億円に達し、年初からの累計は27 兆 1342 億円と暦年で過去最大を更新した。 
その後は円高へと自律的な反発も見られた。9 月2～3 日には、日銀の追加利上げ観測の強まり
や米雇用関連指標の下振れを受けてドル円は1 日で3 円以上下落、9 日には153 円台まで円高が進
んだ。この局面では介入実績は無く、ジャクソンホール会議でのウォーシュFRB議長の発言をきっ

### Page 2

2/6 
かけに積み上がったドル買い・円売りの持ち高が、損切りを迫られた結果と考えられる。 
ところが、その後は再び円安に押し戻された。FRBは 9 月 16 日に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切
り、政策金利を3.75～4.00％とした。日銀も18 日に政策金利を1.25％へ引き上げたものの、日
米がそろって利上げしたことで金利差は大きく変わらず、ドル円は158 円台へと戻っている。 
為替市場における"投機的な動き"の指標とされるのが、円先物市場における非商業部門（主に
ヘッジファンドなどとされる）の建玉である。その推移を見ると、ここ数ヶ月で大きな変化が生
じていることがわかる（図表1）。 
6 月 2 日時点の円売建玉は24 万 4416 枚と、円キャリートレードがブームだった2007 年以来の
高水準に達していた。差引（ネット）でも12 万9567 枚の売り越しだった。その後も、9 月1 日時
点では 9 万2227 枚の売り越しと、円売り超の状態が続いていた。 
しかし、9 月前半の急激な円高を境に状況は一変し、9 月 15 日時点では差引が12 万 0359 枚の
買い越しへと転じ、わずか2 週間で円買い方向に21 万枚強も振れた。この振れ幅は、統計をさか
のぼれる1986 年以降で最大である。同時点の円買建玉（23 万 7951 枚）も過去最大を記録した。 
もっとも、その後は買い越し幅の縮小が続いている。10 月13 日時点では、買建玉19 万 1256 枚
に対し売建玉14 万 1049 枚で、差引の買い越しは5 万0207 枚とピークの半分以下に縮んだ。売建
玉は 9 月末の12 万枚台から14 万枚台へと再び増えており、円売りの持ち高が積み上がり始めて
いる兆しもうかがえる。投資主体別に見ると、レバレッジド・ファンドは10 月 13 日時点で3 万
3673 枚の円売り越しとなっており、ヘッジファンドの円買い意欲がすでに後退していることを示
している。 
足元の売建玉は、過去の介入局面のように当局が介入を急がなければならない水準にはない。
一方で、9 月に積み上がった円の買い持ちの解消そのものが、円安圧力として作用している面もあ
ると考えられる。

2.  実質金利差から見たドル円相場
為替相場の方向性を最終的に決めるのは、日米の金融政策と、それを反映した金利差である。
そこで、日米の実質金利差を、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債の3 つの年限で見てみる（図表2） 。こ
こでは、実質化に用いる物価として、市場が織り込む期待インフレ率であるBEI（ブレーク・イー
ブン・インフレ率、10 年債）を用いた。足元の消費者物価は、米国では原油高で押し上げられ、
日本では電気・ガス料金支援で押し下げられており、実勢を見誤りやすいためである。
名目で見ると、政策金利は米FF金利の誘導目標が3.75～4.00％、日本の無担保コール翌日物が
1.25％である。10 月 21 日時点の国債利回りは、米国が2 年債4.79％・10 年債 5.13％、日本が2
年債 1.886％・10 年債2.882％となっている。一方、10 年BEIは米国が2.35％（10 月20 日） 、日
本が 2.1～2.2％程度と、ほぼ同水準である。これを差し引いて実質化すると、筆者の試算ではお

おむね次のようになる。
✓ 政策金利：米国＋1.5～1.6％程度、日本▲0.9～1.0％程度（米国優位・差は約2.5％ポイント）
✓ 2 年債：米国＋2.4％程度、日本▲0.3％程度（米国優位・差は約2.7％ポイント）
✓ 10 年債：米国＋2.8％程度、日本＋0.7％程度（米国優位・差は約2.1％ポイント）
名目で見れば、日本の10 年債利回りが9 月下旬に3.115％と約 30 年ぶりの水準に達するなど、
日米金利差は縮小してきた。しかし、BEIで実質化すると景色は異なる。すべての年限で米国の実
質金利が2％ポイント以上も上回っており、ドル優位の構図はほとんど崩れていないのである。
その背景には、日米で金利上昇の"中身"が異なることがある。米国では、昨年末から長期金利
が約 1％ポイント上昇したが、BEIは 2.25％から2.35％へとほぼ横ばいにとどまった。名目金利の
注：BEIで実質化
出所：Datastream,Bloomberg

### Page 4

4/6 
上昇は、ほぼそのまま実質金利の上昇となり、10 年物の実質利回りは9 月末に 2.93％と2008 年
以来の高水準に達した。これに対し日本では、BEIが過去最高圏の2％台まで上昇しており、名目
金利の上昇の相応の部分を期待インフレ率の上昇が占めている。実際、9 月の日銀の利上げ後も
BEIはむしろ上昇した。日本の金利上昇は"インフレ期待による金利上昇"の色彩が濃く、実質金利
の上昇は限られていると言えよう。 
日本の政策金利は、実質では依然として▲1％近いマイナス圏にあり、金融環境はなお緩和的
だ。日銀の利上げや介入にもかかわらず円安の流れが変わらなかったのは、こうした実質金利差
の構図で説明できる面が大きい。逆に言えば、日本の期待インフレ率が2％近辺で落ち着いたまま
利上げが進むか、米国の実質金利が低下に転じない限り、本格的な円高への転換は難しいと考え
られる。 
なお、日本の物価連動国債は発行残高が約10 兆円と小さく、BEIには流動性の影響も含まれ
る。政策金利や2 年債を10 年BEIで実質化するのも簡便的な手法である点には留意が必要だ。

3.  中間選挙の結果次第で変わるドルの行方 
11 月3 日には米国の中間選挙が行われる。1934 年以降の23 回の中間選挙のうち、現職大統領
の政党が上下両院とも議席を増やしたのはわずか2 回しかない。今回も、ロイター通信の世論調
査ではトランプ大統領の支持率が過去最低の31％まで落ち込み、投票先では民主党47％、共和党
35％と 12 ポイントの差がついている。選挙予測サイトのディシジョン・デスクHQは、民主党が下
院を制する確率を78％、上院を制する確率を56％と見ている。 
結果ごとに、ドル円相場への影響を整理すると次のようになる。 
① 民主党が上下両院を制する場合：予算や減税、関税政策に対する議会のチェックが強まり、
財政拡張への期待が後退することで、米長期金利の低下とドル安につながりやすい。FRBの人事に
対する議会の影響力が強まり、金融政策の独立性への懸念が和らぐことも、長期金利の押し下げ
要因となろう。一方で、予算案を巡る対立が先鋭化すれば、政府機関の閉鎖などを通じたリスク
回避の円買いが生じる可能性もある。いずれにしても、ドル円には下押し圧力がかかる公算が大
きい。 
② 下院は民主党、上院は共和党の"ねじれ"となる場合：現在の政策の大枠が維持される一方
で、新たな大型財政出動は難しくなる。相場の反応は限定的で、関心は再び日米の金融政策へと
移ることになろう。 
③ 共和党が両院の過半数を維持する場合：減税や規制緩和など拡張的な政策への期待から、米
長期金利の上昇とドル高が進みやすい。ドル円が再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が一気に高
まる展開も想定される。 
世論調査どおりであれば①ないし②の公算が大きく、ドル円にはどちらかと言えば円高寄りの
材料となろう。ただし、重要なのは選挙後の財政・通商政策や金利見通しの変化であり、選挙直
後の値動きだけで方向性を判断するのは早計だろう。

### Page 5

5/6 
4.  イラン情勢：選挙後の"重し"が外れる 
もう一つの大きな不透明要因がイラン情勢である。2 月 28 日に始まった米国・イスラエルとイ
ランの戦闘は、ホルムズ海峡の事実上の封鎖と、米国によるイラン港湾の海上封鎖を伴ったまま
長期化している。北海ブレント原油先物は20 日に一時1 バレル＝111.99 ドルと約5 ヶ月ぶりの高
値をつけた。 
原油高は、いくつかの経路でドル高円安に作用している。米国では、ガソリン価格の前年比
34％の上昇などを背景に、9 月の消費者物価が同＋3.7％へと加速し、FRBの追加利上げ観測を支え
ている。日本にとっては、エネルギー輸入額の増加を通じて貿易収支が悪化し、実需の円売りが
増える。加えて、地政学リスクの高まりは"有事のドル買い"も誘いやすい。 
注目されるのは、トランプ大統領が「中間選挙前にはイランを攻撃しない」と明言している点
だ。言い換えれば、選挙後はこの制約が外れることになる。選挙後に軍事行動が再開されれば、
原油価格の一段高と米利上げ観測の強まりを通じて、ドル高円安が進むリスクがある。逆に、仲
介国を通じた交渉がまとまり、海峡の再開に向かえば、原油価格の下落とともに米長期金利が低
下し、ドル安円高へと振れやすい。実際、20～21 日には原油安を受けて米長期金利が低下し、ド
ルが売られる場面が見られた。

5.  日米の金融政策：12 月の"同時利上げ"の先が焦点 
FRBは9 月に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切り、年内にもう1 回の利上げを示唆した。10 月14 日
に9 月の消費者物価が前年比3.7％と予想を上回ると、27～28 日のFOMCでの利上げ確率は一時5
割前後まで上昇した。しかし、17 日にウォーシュ議長が10 月会合の判断を予断しない姿勢を示し
たことで、利上げ確率は1 割台半ばまで低下している。市場では「10 月は見送り、12 月に利上
げ」との見方が大勢となった。一部には、12 月が今回の利上げ局面で最後になるとの予想も出て
いる。 
一方、日銀の植田総裁は、9 月の利上げ後に「政策の局面は変化した」と述べ、その後も基調的
な物価上昇率が2％に「かなり近づいている」との認識を示した。そのうえで、 「これ以上上がる
ことは容認できない」とも踏み込んでいる。29～30 日の決定会合では政策金利が据え置かれる公
算が大きいが、基調物価が2％に到達したと判断されると報じられている。 
日米がともに12 月に利上げすれば、名目の政策金利差は変わらない。為替にとって重要なの
は、その先の方向性の違いである。FF金利が明確に中立水準を超えるFRBの利上げが最終局面に近
づく一方、日銀は中立金利を目指して利上げを続ける。この構図が鮮明になれば、日米の実質金
利差は徐々に縮小に向かおう。逆に、原油高を受けて米国の物価上昇が加速し、FRBが一段の利上
げを迫られる展開となれば、実質金利差の縮小は見込めず、ドル円相場の膠着状態を演出しよ
う。

6.  今後の見通し：年末に向けて緩やかな円高を想定、ただし分岐は大きい 
以上を踏まえると、当面のドル円相場は、日米の金融政策会合と中間選挙という大きなイベン

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
トを控え、当面は現状の狭いレンジでの推移が続くと考えられる。10 月27～28 日のFOMCは据え置
きが市場の大勢だ。FOMCで利上げが見送られ、その後の日銀会合で基調物価2％到達が確認されて
12 月利上げへの地ならしが進めば、円高方向に振れやすい組み合わせとなる。しかし、市場では
FRBも年内利上げを実施するとの見方が強く、ドル円相場を動かす材料とはなりにくい。逆に、
FRBが10 月のFOMCで利上げを決定した場合、ドル円相場は159 円を超える円安進展の可能性が高
まる。 
年末にかけては、①中間選挙で民主党が議会の主導権を握り、②イラン情勢が交渉による解決
に向かい原油価格が落ち着き、③日銀が12 月に利上げを実施して来年以降も利上げ継続の姿勢を
示す、という三つの条件がそろえば、日米の実質金利差は縮小に向かう。その場合は、150 円台半
ばから前半への円高が進む可能性がある。投機筋の売建玉が再び積み上がっている局面であれ
ば、その巻き戻しが円高の勢いを増幅することも考えられる。 
一方で、①共和党の善戦、②選挙後のイランへの軍事行動の再開、③FRBの利上げ継続＋日銀の
利上げ慎重姿勢打ち出し、といった組み合わせとなれば、ドル円は再び160 円台を試すことにな
ろう。その場合、政府・日銀は過去の介入水準である160 円台前半から164 円近辺を意識しつ
つ、円安のスピードを抑えるための介入に踏み切る公算が大きい。ただし、これまでの経験が示
すように、ファンダメンタルズが変わらない中での介入は"時間を買う"政策にすぎず、効果が一
時的にとどまる可能性には注意が必要だ。 
筆者は、①米中間選挙は最低でも上下院のねじれ、場合によっては上院においても民主党の過
半数獲得、②選挙後に米国がイランへの軍事行動を再開、同時に仲介国を介した交渉を再開する
ものの、膠着状態が続く、③日米とも利上げ継続、をメインシナリオとしている。 
さらにリスク要因として、①米中間選挙の結果がなかなか確定せず、議会勢力の確定に時間が
かかる、②イラン情勢はフーシ派から攻撃を受けているサウジアラビアや、総選挙を終えたイス
ラエルによる本格的な攻撃参戦、の可能性を挙げる。 
このように不透明要因が多いことも足元のドル円相場の膠着の要因になっていると考えられる
が、短期的な値動きに一喜一憂するよりも、実質金利差という基本的な決定要因がどちらに動い
ているかを冷静に見極めることが重要であり、日米中央銀行の金融政策姿勢が最も注目される。

以 上

## Document 024: 2026 has exposed new risks for the European gas market (2026-11-09)

Report source: BNP Paribas

Pages: 3

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
3
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.41 / 9 November 2026EDITORIAL
2026 HAS EXPOSED NEW RISKS FOR THE EUROPEAN GAS MARKET
1 C. Grekou, V. Mignon, L. Ragot, 2026, ‘From Russian gas to the global LNG market: The European Union’s new vulnerability’, CEPII, Policy Brief.
2 ACER, 2026, ‘Analysis of the European LNG market development. Monitoring report’.
3 These contracts allow the buyer to choose, or change, the delivery point for the shipment. Recent US contracts (Free On Board and flexible destination) generally

feature these characteristics.
4 IEA, 2026, ‘Global Gas Security Review & Gas Market Report Q4-2026’.
Europe’s vulnerability to the gas market has changed in recent years. Whilst its reliance on imports has remained stable,
its gas supply has been more vulnerable to global market conditions since 2022, due to a shift in the composition of its
imports. Furthermore, in 2026, the combination of high geopolitical risk in one of the main production regions and the

pressure of climate change on European consumers has created an unprecedented situation, leading to price pressures
and significant uncertainty regarding the short-term outlook. Even if they ease, tensions in the European gas market could
persist beyond the winter, largely due to the need to refill inventories.
Whilst Europe is not heavily reliant on gas imports from the Middle
East, it has had to contend with a fresh surge in prices since the out -

break of war in Iran last February, a rise that has, in part, been passed
on to electricity prices this summer. Furthermore, new sources of
vulnerability, particularly those linked to climate change, are at play.
These call for caution regarding the short-term outlook for the Euro -
pean gas market.
EUROPE’S GROWING EXPOSURE TO MARKET VOLATILITY
Unlike the 2022 energy crisis, which hit Europe directly, the war in
Iran is indirectly affecting the European gas market. 80% of gas exports

from the Middle East are destined for Asia. However, the growing inter-
connection between the two main gas-importing regions — Asia and
Europe — has resulted in the crisis spreading to Europe. Since 2022,
the transmission of shocks to liquefied natural gas (LNG) benchmark
prices on the European and Asian markets (TTF and JKM) has increased
significantly, in both directions1.
Despite a fall of around 20% in European gas consumption since 2022,
the replacement of around 60% of Russian pipeline imports with pur -

chases of liquefied natural gas (see Chart 1) has kept Europe’s vulne -
rability at a high level. Given developments in the LNG market —
growing interconnection between European and Asian markets and
the importance of spot contracts (around one-third of European supply
contracts2) — Europe finds itself, through no fault of its own, increa -
singly exposed to gas price volatility.
Furthermore, the increasing autonomy and flexibility of the global
LNG market may exacerbate this volatility. The higher proportion of

LNG sales contracts with flexible destinations3 (rising from 29% to 51%
of the total between 2016 and 2025), linked to the massive influx of
US LNG onto the market, together with the reduction in the share of
contracts indexed to oil prices (from 70% to 55% over the same period),
have made price formation on the LNG market more autonomous, the-
reby increasing the opportunities for arbitrage and the flexibility of this
market.
MAJOR DISRUPTIONS IN THE LNG MARKET HAVE CONSTRAINED THE REFIL-

LING OF EUROPEAN INVENTORIES
The 85% reduction in LNG exports from Qatar and the UAE in Q2 and Q3
2026 was offset by two-thirds through, on the one hand, an increase
in exports from other producing countries (primarily the United States)
and, on the other hand, a fall in demand in Asia and Europe (which
is forecast to reach, respectively, 1.5% and 3% year-on-year in 2026,
according to IEA forecasts4). Owing to the damage caused by the war to
infrastructure (17% of Qatar’s production capacity is out of service) and

logistical constraints specific to the LNG market (unavailability of LNG
carriers), the LNG market has benefited only marginally from the re -
sumption of flows through the Strait of Hormuz since July. Against this
backdrop of tensions in the gas market, high prices in Europe (up by
around 174% since the outbreak of the conflict) have significantly
slowed the refilling of European stocks. They stood at 75% of full capa-
city at the end of October, whilst the official target is 80% (see Chart 2).

OVER THE WINTER, DIVERGENT FACTORS COULD STABILISE PRICES AT A
HIGH LEVEL
Gas stock levels that are below the target set by the European Commis-
sion do not increase the risk of a shortage on the European continent.
Nevertheless, Europe’s vulnerability towards the end of winter, in the
event of a cold snap, would force it to buy on the market at high prices.

Furthermore, from a technical perspective, exposure to the conse -
quences of a cold snap at the end of winter is all the more pronounced
as the rate at which gas can be withdrawn from storage (the ease with
which a country can access its supplies) decreases (due to lower stock
levels in storage facilities).
Apart from stock levels at the start of winter, three factors will deter -

mine the situation at the end of the season, a key period of vulnera -
bility:
1/ the resumption of exports from the Gulf, a region that is essential for
restoring market balance in the short term;
2/ weather conditions during the winter;
3/ renewable energy production.
According to the IEA, in the worst-case scenario (with exports from the
Middle East only resuming from the end of the first quarter of 2027, a
cold winter and low renewable energy production), stocks would stand

at 7% of total capacity by the end of winter, compared with 33% in
the best-case scenario. Such levels remain significantly below the ave-
rage for the last five years at this time of year: 80% in the worst-case
scenario and 18% in the best-case scenario. This would underpin res -
tocking demand over the next two quarters, and therefore keep prices
at a fairly high level. For the time being, a significant recovery in LNG
exports from the Gulf remains highly uncertain, even though indirect

talks between the warring parties have resumed. On a more positive
note, the current development of a very powerful El Niño event should,
in principle, be associated with relatively mild temperatures during the
winter in western Europe. According to the futures markets, European
gas prices are not expected to return to their pre-crisis levels until
2028 (see Chart 3).
ARE WE HEADING FOR A SUMMER PEAK IN GAS DEMAND?
In addition to these factors, which are likely to prolong market tensions

during the first half of 2027, the summer period could also be cha -
racterised by increased demand for gas. During the summer of 2026,
5 In early October 2026, the water levels in French reservoirs reached a ten-year low, standing at 23% below seasonal averages.
6 RTE data.
7 M. Mueller, P . Devaux, October 2026, ‘What lessons can we learn to keep European industry competitive from the measures taken by Germany, as the cost of electricity
continues to turn the screw?’, EcoInsight.

8 Oxford Institute for Energy Studies, October 2026, Quarterly Gas Market Review.
weather conditions hampered the generation of certain forms of re -
newable energy (heatwaves and droughts restricting electricity gene -
ration from hydroelectric5 and wind power sources). They also disrup-
ted nuclear power generation (in France, nuclear electricity generation
fell by around 10% year-on-year between August and October6). Along-
side these constraints on carbon-free electricity generation, heatwaves

are driving up demand for electricity, particularly for air conditioning.
Against this backdrop, as a source of flexibility within the electricity
mix, gas remains a key determinant of electricity prices in Europe 7.
In Q3 2026, whilst the decline in European gas consumption mainly
affected industry, the residential sector and the commercial sector 8,
consumption by gas-fired power stations was 9% higher than in 2025.
STRENGTHENING EUROPEAN PROGRAMMES

In this context, and despite the progress made in decarbonising the
European electricity mix, high gas prices have led to a rise in whole -
sale electricity prices in most European countries. Between July and
September 2026, these prices were on average 54% higher than in the
same period a year earlier.
Against this backdrop of widespread rises in energy prices, European go-
vernments have primarily introduced measures to support households’
purchasing power when buying fuel. Measures relating to gas supply

remain fairly limited. The European Commission has extended the tar-
get for refilling European stocks to 80% (instead of 90%) for 2026. It has
also urged Member States to extend the stock-refill season in order
to smooth out purchase prices. Furthermore, the measures taken in
2022 under the RePower EU programme have been maintained: redu -
cing dependence on Russian gas with the aim of a complete phase-out
(pipeline and LNG) by the end of 2027; and pooling gas purchases at

European level. The ‘decarbonisation and electrification’ component of
this programme was strengthened in April 2026 by the Accelerate EU
programme, notably through measures to accelerate the electrification
of demand and the development of electricity grid infrastructure at
European level.

At national level, it is worth highlighting the price cap mechanism for
gas purchased by power stations, which the Italian government is set
to introduce. In 2025, gas set the price of electricity in Italy in 73% of
cases (compared with an average of 40% across Europe). This price cap
mechanism, similar to that introduced in Spain in 2022, is, in principle,

only sustainable in the short term. Indeed, its cost is likely to be signi-
ficant given the Italian electricity mix’s high dependence on gas.
*****
Increased market dependence and the consequences of rising climate
risk for European electricity generation are altering the vulnerabilities
associated with the continent’s gas market. The sources of risk are
increasing in number and becoming harder to manage. This calls for
the continuation and strengthening of decarbonisation efforts in the

European policies launched in 2022.

MSCI 降評風險升高，印尼股市保守看待
2026 年 10 月 27 日
一、 市場動態
1. MSCI 擬將印尼降至獨立市場，降評風險明顯升高：MSCI 於 10 月 15 日宣布，啟動
將印尼股市由「新興市場（Emerging Market）」直接降至「獨立市場（Standalone
Market） 」的諮詢程序，意見徵詢將於2027 年 4 月 30 日截止，最晚於 2027 年 6 月
30 日公布最終決定 。MSCI 原先於今年 1 月提出可能降至 「邊境市場 （Frontier Market
） 」 ，此次則跳過「邊境市場」提出直接將該國股市降至「獨立市場」的方案，顯示對

印尼市場治理的疑慮進一步加深。雖然「邊境市場」仍可能是替代分類方案，但MSCI
強調，除非有明確證據顯示市場治理及運作出現實質改善，否則預期將依目前提案重
新分類，顯示此次降評風險較過去顯著升高。
2. 股權透明度與交易秩序問題為 MSCI 主要疑慮：MSCI 指出，印尼股市長期存在股權
結構不透明、實際自由流通股比例難以確認、疑似協調交易，以及市場監理執法成效
不足等問題，影響國際投資人對市場公平性與可投資性的信心。雖然印尼主管機關已
提出強化持股超過 1%股東資訊揭露、建立高持股集中度（HSC）監理機制，以及規
劃逐步將最低自由流通股比例提高至 15%等改革措施，但MSCI 認為相關改革尚未展
現持續且具實質性的改善成果。由於問題涉及市場制度與治理結構，改善所需時間可
能較一般短期市場波動更長。
3. 股市反彈後再度回落，外資撤離壓力仍高：受降評消息衝擊，印尼雅加達綜合指數 10
月 16 日重挫 11.57%，創 1998 年以來最大單日跌幅，19 日再跌 3.64%，盤中最低
觸及 4,907.37 點 。 雖然20 日至 23 日連續四個交易日反彈 ， 但26 日再度下跌 1.22%

，收於 5,373.09 點，各產業類股全面收黑，顯示市場信心仍不穩定。根據 Reuters
報導，截至 10 月 19 日，2026 年外資已累計淨賣超印尼股票約 48.8 億美元。此外，
印尼證交所 26 日宣布，將引進獨立專家及外國投資人代表參與高持股集中度審查，
並自 11 月起加強調查疑似協調交易，必要時凍結可疑帳戶，以回應 MSCI 對市場制
度的疑慮。
二、 下跌原因分析：
1. 股市：潛在指數剔除與資金外流，持續壓抑市場評價
若印尼股市最終降至「獨立市場」， 原則上將不再納入MSCI 新興市場及邊境市場相關
指數，追蹤相關指數的被動式基金將須依規則調整持股，實際處理方式仍以 MSCI 後
續公告為準。若改降至「邊境市場」，則可能獲得部分邊境市場資金承接，衝擊相對較
小。Reuters 引述多家金融機構估計，印尼股市降至獨立市場可能引發約 30 億至 60

2. 匯率：外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力
印尼股市的降評風險不僅衝擊股市，也可能透過外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力。印尼
盾 10 月 16 日盤中一度貶至 18,070 兌 1 美元，為 7 月下旬以來最弱水準之一，當日
收盤約 18,000。若後續國際資金持續流出，可能進一步增加股匯市波動，對以外幣計
價、且未完全避險的印尼基金及 ETF 形成額外壓力。雖然印尼央行可透過匯市干預及

流動性管理措施穩定金融市場，但在資金外流壓力尚未緩解前，印尼盾仍可能維持偏
弱格局。
3. 基金與 ETF：指數權重下滑，單一市場曝險風險升高
MSCI 相關指數調整已對印尼股票產生實質影響。Reuters 引用 LSEG 資料顯示，印尼
在 MSCI 新興市場指數的權重已由 2026 年初的 1.16%降至 9 月底的 0.43%，相關成
分股亦由年初 18 檔減至 9 檔，反映部分指數資金調整壓力已提前出現。對印尼單一
國家基金及 ETF 而言，主要風險來自股市下跌、資金流出及印尼盾波動；主動式基金
雖可透過選股降低部分影響，但仍難以完全迴避整體市場風險。不同產品實際受到的
影響，仍應依追蹤指數、主要持股及匯率避險安排個別評估。
三、 市場看法：
1. 制度性問題難以速解，降評風險將持續牽動市場：我們認為，此次 MSCI 降評風險並
非單純反映經濟成長放緩或企業獲利不佳，而是涉及市場透明度、交易公平性與監理
執法等結構性問題。尤其 MSCI 不僅啟動降評諮詢，更持續限制印尼股票的部分指數

調整，包括不提高外資納入因子、不新增相關成分股，以及不進行向上規模分類調整
，顯示市場可投資性疑慮尚未獲得有效解決。儘管政府試圖推動改革，但制度修正仍
須經過執行與成效驗證，才能重新取得國際投資人的信任，因此短期內降評風險仍難
完全消除。
2. 外資撤離與指數權重下降，市場信心修復恐需較長時間：印尼今年以來外資已大幅賣
超，MSCI 新興市場指數中的權重亦明顯下降，顯示國際投資人對當地市場的疑慮已
逐漸反映於實際資金流向。我們認為，即使印尼最終成功避免降評，部分國際機構投
資人仍可能因市場透明度、流動性及治理品質疑慮而維持較低曝險。此外，MSCI 對
部分指數調整的凍結措施尚未解除，短期內新增指數資金流入機會仍受到限制，預期
國際資金回流與市場評價修復均可能需要較長時間。
3. 監理改革力度加大，但尚不足以扭轉保守展望：印尼政府近期持續加強改革，包括提
高資訊透明度、強化交易監控、對違規交易開罰，以及引進外部獨立人士參與監理審
查。10 月 26 日該國政府進一步提出主動調查疑似協調交易及凍結可疑帳戶等措施，

11 月 2 日起亦規劃將高持股集中度股票排除於雅加達綜合指數之外 。 相關改革有助改

### Page 3

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
善交易秩序及短期市場信心，但部分措施仍待正式實施，且實際成效尚未獲得 MSCI
認可。考量制度性問題改善需要時間，政策利多雖可能帶動短線反彈，但尚不足以改
變對印尼股市中期前景的保守判斷。
4. 股市跌深不代表利空出盡，中期風險報酬吸引力偏低：印尼股市在 10 月 16 日及 19
日大幅修正後，雖曾連續四個交易日反彈，但 26 日再度回落，顯示市場尚未形成穩
定回升趨勢。我們認為，當前股市面臨的不僅是短期賣壓，更包括國際投資人信任下
降及風險溢價提高，即使企業基本面未同步惡化，仍可能因評價下修而承受股價壓力

。因此，短線不排除政策利多或跌深後出現技術性反彈，但在 MSCI 態度、外資流向
與印尼盾走勢明顯改善前，反彈延續性仍有疑慮，印尼股市中期展望維持保守。
四、市場展望：
1. 印尼股市維持保守展望，單一市場風險報酬吸引力不足：考量 MSCI 降評風險尚未解除、
外資撤離壓力持續 ， 以及市場治理改革仍有待驗證 ， 我們認為印尼股市未來數季的風險報
酬吸引力相對有限。即使近期股價明顯回落，仍難認定相關風險已充分反映。相較市場制
度成熟、國際資金流動性較佳的新興市場，印尼股市可能持續面臨較高風險溢價，因此對
單一印尼市場的集中曝險應維持審慎態度。
2. 此事件對新興市場指數與東協指數影響相對有限：根據 MSCI 資料，截至2026 年 9 月底
，印尼股市在 MSCI Emerging Market 指數與 MSCI AC ASEAN 指數中的權重分別約為
0.43%與 7.55%，因此我們認為印尼潛在降評對相關指數的直接衝擊相對有限。此外，新
興市場各國經濟結構與產業組成有所差異 ， 區域型基金可透過跨國分散降低單一市場風險

。惟後續仍須留意印尼事件對整體新興市場投資信心造成的外溢影響 ， 以及全球貿易與匯
率波動風險。

市場焦點
美國
• 9月CPI年增3.7%、PPI年增6.3%，雙雙高於前期 ，能源價格的急升是通膨回升的主要推力 。
雖然核心CPI年增率僅升至2.6%，但住房與服務價格仍維持黏著，使通膨降溫過程出現反覆；
同時PPI增幅明顯高於 CPI，反映企業成本壓力偏高 ，未來不排除進一步向終端價格傳導 。整
體而言，CPI與PPI同步回升反映通膨降溫進程仍存在不確定性 ，進而令市場重估通膨回落速
度與對Fed貨幣政策路徑的審慎預期。
AI
• AI雲端業者 CoreWeave的5年期CDS利差一度突破 1,140個基點 ，旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近
15%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延至股票市場 。全球最大半導體設備商ASML表示，客戶已開始洽
談2029至2030年的訂單；晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期 。與此同時，台灣出口增

速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季
相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。
中國
• 9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及 AI硬體需求，能源和科技領域之外 ，普遍性的通縮壓力
仍在，彭博表示以GDP平減指數衡量 ，經歷三年下滑後 ，整體經濟預計將在 2026年走出通縮 ，
投資上短期可聚焦，受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。

美股獲利引擎轉向半導體/核心AI與能源
• 雖然標普500指數在2026年第三季展現出獲利韌性 ，但隨著油價上漲侵蝕利潤率 、消費者受通膨壓抑 ，以及分析師下調未
來預測動能，市場未來的容錯空間正在縮小。
• AI與半導體仍為獲利領頭羊：科技與半導體持續為標普500指數獲利成長的核心動力，其中約20家企業組成的核心AI一籃子
股票(權重約34.6%)對指數獲利的貢獻度已超過科技七雄(Mag 7)，且此優勢預計將至少延續至2028年底。
• 油價推升能源產業，接棒成為強棒：受到近期國際油價上揚影響，能源產業獲利預測顯著上修，預計獲利成長勁道將延續至
2027年初。在接下來的三個季度中，能源板塊的收益成長表現預計將超越科技七雄。
• 消費與運輸業面臨成本壓頂：相較於科技與能源的強勁，必需消費品與交通運輸業正承受顯著壓力。例如，達美航空受高燃

料成本拖累，而Nike與百事可樂則反映出消費者因持續通膨而縮減支出的困境。
7月以來EPS和營收成長預期修正

利潤率高檔盤整、關稅退款帶來短補，但動能已見放緩
• 利潤率高檔盤整，淨利潤率提升受限：標普500成分股第二季營運利潤率(18.7%)與淨利潤率(15.6%)均創下新高。雖然第三
季營運利潤率預估可進一步提升至19.4%，但淨利潤率預計微幅上升至15.8%，顯示整體企業淨利潤的擴張動能正漸趨平緩 。
• 700億美元關稅退款提供短線補藥： 受惠於美國政府依據國際緊急經濟權力法 (IEEPA)的追償退稅，估計仍有至少700億美元
的關稅款將退還給標普 500成分股(主要集中於工業、非必需消費及醫療保健等 )，這預計能為2026年標普500指數每股收益
(EPS)成長帶來約235個基點、利潤率提升約34個基點的短期提振。
• 上修動能開始轉弱：雖然整體財報指引動量仍維持正值，但過去六週內，分析師對未來12個月EPS與收入的修正動能出現頻

繁下修，這意味著市場先前的極度樂觀情緒正在消退，未來財報季度要優於預期的門檻正在提高。
• 小結與投資建議： 標普500指數短期內靠著半導體 /AI與能源雙引擎拉抬 ，加上關稅退款的一次性利多注入 ，下半年整體獲
利依然具備支撐。然而，高油價帶來的營運成本上升 ，以及終端消費力道的疲軟 ，正逐漸侵蝕非科技/非能源企業的利潤 ，
而大盤廣度不足(僅少數板塊支撐獲利)，代表未來投資風險正逐漸集中 ，但AI算力需求仍屬硬性需求，但波動加劇下，投資
建議以定期定額方式投資美股核心AI與半導體龍頭股票。
利潤率預測
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.16

美國9月CPI、PPI 同步升溫，能源衝擊擴大通膨與利率風險
• 能源價格急升，消費通膨再升溫：美國9月CPI月增0.6%、
年增3.7%，高於前月 3.4%，其中汽油月增 9.1%、能源
價格月增 5.2%，汽油單項即貢獻整體 CPI當月漲幅逾半；
另一方面，核心CPI月增0.3%、年增2.6%、住房指數月
增0.4%，顯示能源雖是本次通膨回升主因 ，但住房及部
分服務價格仍具黏著性，令通膨降溫進程面臨反覆。
• 上游價格大幅攀升 ，成本傳導風險升高： 9月PPI最終需
求月增1.3%、年增6.3%，創2022年3月以來最大增幅，
其中最終需求商品月增 2.6%，能源價格大漲 10.8%，汽
油價格更跳升 20.3%；核心PPI月增0.5%、年增5.0%，
顯示價格壓力不僅來自能源 ，也涉及部分商品與服務環

節；後續是否進一步推升至 CPI，仍須觀察企業成本轉
嫁程度及需求變化。
• 市場看法： 通膨回升恐提高 Fed再度升息機率 ，並延長
高利率維持時間 ，甚至不排除進一步緊縮的可能 ，短期
有利支撐美債殖利率及美元表現 ，不過股、債市後續走
勢仍將受到貨幣政策預期與風險情緒變化影響 。資產配
置建議可關注具成本轉嫁能力 、現金流穩健的能源類股；
債券則宜以短中天期 、高信用評等標的為主 ，以降低利
率敏感度。黃金可作為分散地緣政治及通膨風險的輔助
配置，但不宜將任何單一資產視為必然受惠標的。
CPI 與核心 CPI 走勢變動

供給面推動中國9月CPI升0.9%
• 供給面推動中國9月通膨復甦：中國9月通膨數據顯示，CPI年增率由8月的0.8%升至0.9%，連續兩個月擴大，但低於市場預期
的1.0%，物價上漲主要受到能源及旅遊價格帶動，而食品價格跌幅進一步擴大，使整體通膨壓力仍相對溫和，扣除食品與能源
後，核心CPI維持在1.0%，反映內需需求尚未明顯升溫 。另一方面，PPI年增率由3.8%大幅升至5.0%，優於市場預期，且月增
率達1.2%，創4月以來最大漲幅，顯示上游原物料及工業品價格回升，須關注未來是否逐步傳導至消費端價格。
• 展望後市： 9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及 AI硬體需求 ，能源和科技領域之外 ，普遍性的通縮壓力仍 在，彭博表示 以
GDP平減指數衡量，經歷三年下滑後，整體經濟預計將在2026年走出通縮，值得注意的是，這種供給面驅動的通膨復甦，將降

低官方推出需求刺激措施的緊迫感 ，而要反轉通縮循環正需要官方舉措，投資上短期可聚焦受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的
半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.14
通膨集中於能源和AI相關行業

過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數
的兩倍
• 過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數的兩倍：主因為獲利帶動成長，整體而言，新興市場企
業獲利表現強勁 ，而且仍持續加速成長 ，高盛預估2026年新興市場企業盈餘成長率將達 55%，超過標普500企業盈餘成長速
度的兩倍，且新興市場股票估值較美國股市存在超過40%的折價空間，評價相對具吸引力，今年以來流入新興市場股市的資金
已接近500億美元，創下過去20年來最強勁紀錄之一。更重要的是新興市場國家目前貢獻了全球近 80%的AI硬體出口。隨著AI
產業持續發展，受惠範圍有望從少數龍頭企業擴大至更多國家及公司，特別是那些能夠協助解決供應鏈瓶頸的企業。
• 結論：後市來看在AI產業趨勢、相對低估的評價水準，以及強勁企業獲利成長三大因素支撐下，新興市場股票未來一年仍具備

進一步上漲的潛力，有機會延續目前的強勢表現。

AI投資支撐美國成長，融資需求升高使長端利率與信用風險並存
• AI基礎建設持續擴張 ，成為企業投資重要動能 ：大型雲端業者
持續投入資料中心 、半導體、網路設備與電力基礎設施 ，帶動
相關產業資本支出與供應鏈需求 。摩根大通資產管理指出 ，納
入資料中心開發商及半導體投資後，AI 基礎建設至 2030 年的
累計投資需求可能達約 5兆美元 ，顯示AI投資循環仍具規模 ，
但實際經濟貢獻與企業獲利 ，仍取決於投資效率及商業化成果 。
• 外部融資需求增加 ，債券供給與投資回報成關鍵： 隨AI投資規
模擴大，大型科技企業逐步增加公司債與外部融資 ，在企業債
與公債供給同步增加下 ，長天期利率面臨上行壓力 。若未來AI
獲利成長未能跟上資本支出擴張速度 ，可能壓縮自由現金流與

資本報酬率，進一步擴大信用利差 ，並提高高估值科技股的評
價修正風險
• 市場觀察：AI基礎建設仍是未來數年推動全球科技投資與企業
數位轉型的成長主軸，相關需求具備中長期發展空間，而2026
年市場已逐漸轉向檢視投資回報率 、現金流創造能力及融資結
構是否具備可持續性 ，因此單純依賴資本支出擴張已不足以支
撐所有AI概念股評價 。建議配置可兼顧具獲利與現金流支撐的
AI供應鏈，以及高信用評等 、中短天期債券；對長天期債券則
須評估供給與利率風險 ，並審慎檢視高負債 AI企業的償債能力
及投資回報。
資料來源：Goldman Sachs，永豐金證券， 2026.10.12

AI熱潮、高債務與網路威脅，IMF示警三大金融穩定風險升高
資料來源：Bloomberg Finance L.P， Dealogic，Zero day Clock and IMF staff calculations.， 2026.10.13
AI相關風險持續升溫 主權債券殖利率攀升 網路資安威脅升高
大型雲端科技企業債券發行規模
（占投資等級公司債市場比重) 10年期公債殖利率平均變動幅度 零時差漏洞占比（%）
• AI估值高度集中，市場重定價風險升溫：IMF於10月《全球金融穩定報告》指出，AI相關投資已成為全球成長的重要支撐，
但資金與漲勢高度集中於少數企業及市場，一旦獲利成長不如預期或估值遭重新檢視，可能引發科技股修正並透過風險偏好
下降擴散至整體金融市場。
• 通膨與財政疑慮恐推升債券市場波動： IMF認為，若能源價格推升通膨壓力或市場對財政永續性產生疑慮 ，主權債殖利率可

能進一步上升，進而提高全球融資成本與資產重估風險；在高利率與高債務環境下，金融條件快速緊縮的情況將明顯提高。
• 網路安全風險成為潛在威脅： 隨金融體系數位化程度提高 ，IMF將大型網路攻擊列為重要風險來源之一 。若攻擊事件波及支
付系統、金融基礎設施或雲端服務平台，不僅可能影響市場運作效率，也可能透過信心衝擊與流動性壓力擴大金融市場波動。
• 投資建議：市場成長動能仍受AI支撐，但在金融穩定風險上升情境下，建議採取「AI受惠股＋高品質債券＋防禦型資產」的
槓鈴策略；核心配置可聚焦全球AI基礎建設供應鏈及投資等級債，藉由企業長期獲利成長與債券避險功能，同時因應估值修
正及金融條件收緊風險。

AI隱憂浮現但動能仍在
• CoreWeave信用風險亮紅燈：市場憂心雲端業者在算力租賃業務產生足夠回報前，恐難以負擔龐大債務，導致信用風險升
溫。上週，AI雲端業者CoreWeave的5年期信用違約交換（CDS）利差一度突破1,140個基點，升至通常反映嚴重信用壓力
的水準；旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近15%，其中2032年到期的無擔保債券於上週三跌破面值的80%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延
至股票市場。
• AI半導體產業的基本面仍具韌性：全球最大半導體設備商艾司摩爾（ASML）表示，客戶已開始洽談2029至2030年的訂單；
晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期。與此同時，台灣出口增速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯
示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。

美銀美林調查指出，全球經理人對景氣與股市仍維持偏多立場
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10
• 經理人樂觀情緒維持高檔： 美銀美林10月全球基金經理人調查顯示 ，市場風險偏好仍高 ，平均現金水位維持在 3.9%的低檔，
科技股配置則升至2020年8月以來最高，反映經理人對AI產業及企業獲利前景仍具信心 。然而，整體經濟成長預期已轉趨保
守，淨4%的受訪者預期未來 12個月經濟成長放緩 。極端風險方面，債券殖利率無序攀升仍居首位 ，占比31%；AI泡沫及通
膨再起分別為23%及20%。此外，39%的經理人認為，大型雲端業者資本支出是系統性信用事件的主要潛在來源 ，顯示市場
焦點已從AI需求成長延伸至龐大資本支出的獲利回收能力。

• 美國公債殖利率走高 ，債券的收益吸引力逐漸回升： 調查顯示，37%的受訪者表示，當美國10年期公債殖利率升至 6%時，
才會考慮由股票轉向債券；另有19%認為目前殖利率已具吸引力，顯示部分經理人已開始重新評估固定收益資產的配置價值 。
不過，債券整體仍處淨減碼，且經理人對科技股的配置持續升高 ，反映市場尚未全面轉向防禦。後續仍須關注美債殖利率變
化、AI資本支出的投資回報，以及企業獲利能否支撐偏高的市場評價。
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10
基金經理人現金水位 最可能影響市場表現的極端風險

台積電財報佳卻未激勵股價，短線面臨總經干擾
• 台積電Q3營收、獲利及EPS同步改寫單季新高：營收年增50.9%、季增17.6%，稅後純益7,630.6億元，年增68.7％、季增8
％；每股稅後純益(EPS)29.43元，整體優於市場預期，但毛利率66.4％較第二季的67.7％下降。
• 財測展望：第四季財測營收預估 523~543億美元，季增上看15％、年增65％，毛利率預估65%~67%，反映高階晶片需求
依然強勁。全年展望方面，預期2026年美元營收年增約44%，較上季預估的「略高於40%」說法更加明確。此外，重申全
年資本支出600~640億美元不變。
• 市場反應：台積電法說會隔日股價小幅下跌，法說會未出現激勵行情，主要原因包含美國通膨升溫壓力、外資連續調節部位，

以及毛利率略為下降。短線籌碼面可能仍會調整，但台積電董事長魏哲家在法說會中強調，第二波AI需求正強勁興起，台積
電正透過多種途徑全力擴充產能，顯示高階晶片前景明確，中長期具投資價值，多家投資機構也紛紛上調目標價。
資料來源：台積電法說會，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.15。
法說會重點整理 說明
AI需求持續升溫
魏哲家坦言，目前仍難以判斷晶圓供需缺口將擴大或縮小，因為
客戶需求持續增加，台積電也正積極提高產能。儘管消費性電子
市場仍受到零組件漲價及總體經濟不確定性影響，但先進製程需
求依然強勁，公司預估2026年全年美元營收將年增約44％。
擴廠進度
(1)台南科學園區新建3奈米廠預計2027年下半年量產，並決定在
台南再增建一座3奈米廠，規畫2028年投入量產。
(2)美國亞利桑那州第二座晶圓廠已完成廠房興建，預計2027年
第三季量產，時程較原先規畫略為提前；日本第二座晶圓廠則將
導入3奈米製程，預計2028年量產。
台積電證實洽談Terafab
合作
魏哲家強調，目前相關合作仍在討論階段，尚未有結論。不過，

無論未來採取何種合作模式，晶圓廠都必須由台積電負責營運。
電力供應疑慮
美國電力供應情況是公司評估AI長期需求的重要因素之一，因此
台積電進一步確認當地電力供應狀況，目前相關研究及評估結果
均良好，尚未發現明顯問題。

最大的15天
• 全球經濟正處於「韌性仍在、風險升高」的階段：國際貨幣基金組織(IMF)指出，AI投資熱潮持續推動美國、台灣等國家成
長，但中東地緣政治衝突、高通膨風險、政府債務問題與AI泡沫化疑慮，也同時為市場埋下不確定因素。
• 市場永遠充滿雜音，定期定額紀律投資更具優勢：本許多人認為定期定額的核心在於「平均成本」，但更重要的是幫助投資
人克服人性。市場上漲時持續投入，可以參與企業獲利成長帶來的成果，市場下跌時持續扣款，則能以較低價格累積更多單
位數。換句話說，定期定額讓投資人不需要每天判斷：明天會不會跌？現在是不是太貴？要不要先停扣觀望？長期而言，紀

律投資往往比短期擇時的成效更佳。
• AI時代更適合定期定額：儘管IMF認為人工智慧發展為全球經濟成長帶來貢獻，但同時也警告，若市場對AI未來獲利能力過
度樂觀，不排除出現股市估值修正。根據歷史經驗，每一次市場重大修正往往也孕育下一輪成長契機，不論AI發展是否出現
泡沫，善用定期定額策略可以在機會與風險之間達到平衡。
韌性仍在、風險升溫，紀律投資更穩健
任一月份定期定額進場投資試算

原幣計算。指數資料試算僅供參考，非投資商品之實際績效。資料來源Bloomberg。

2. 本資料提及之經濟走勢預測不必然代表基金之績效，基金投資風險請詳閱基金公開說明書。
3. 基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益 ，投資人申購
前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

## Document 140: 261109_daiwa_ Taiwan Consumer Discretionary Global PMX

Source PDF: all report pdf/261109_daiwa_ Taiwan Consumer Discretionary Global PMX.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: We attended PMX’s investor conference after the company 
released its 3Q26 results on 9 November 2026. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call 
as we see better-than-expected demand for its semiconductor, AI server 
and medical segments.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results missed mainly on FX loss. PMX’s 
3Q26 revenue of TWD2,417m (+5.9% QoQ, +24.6% YoY) beat our 
estimate by 8.5% and was in line with the Bloomberg consensus. Its gross 
margin came in at 23.9% vs. 24.6% in 2Q26 and 21.7% in 3Q25, missing 
our estimate (24.2%) and the consensus (25.1%), mainly due to some one-
off costs ramping up at the new Vietnam plant. With higher-than-expected 
opex, operating profit of TWD340m (+10.0% QoQ, +54.4% YoY) missed 
our and the consensus estimates by 1.3% and 6.0%, respectively. With an 
FX loss of TWD23m, its net profit of TWD271m (+13.3% QoQ, +0.1% YoY) 
missed our and consensus estimates by 11.1% and 10.2%, respectively. 
EPS was TWD2.36 in 3Q26. PMX’s 3Q26 revenue breakdown is as 
follows: auto sales at 49% (+13% YoY), semiconductor, AI server & other 
sales at 20% (+66% YoY), storage device sales at 13% (+25% YoY), 
medical sales at 10% (+13% YoY), and sports sales at 8% (+42% YoY). 
PMX also posted October 2026 monthly sales of TWD816m (+3.3% MoM 
and +33.8% YoY; +22.0% YoY for 10M26), which accounted for 35.6% and 
31.3%, respectively, of our previous and the consensus forecasts of 4Q26 
revenue.

Segment transformation continues with upbeat guidance for 4Q26 
and 2027. For 2026, PMX maintained its overall 20-25% YoY revenue 
growth guidance with semiconductor, AI server & others segment growing 
by at least 100% YoY. For 2027, management expects revenue to grow by 
20%+ YoY with the semiconductor, AI server & others segment to grow by 
100%+ YoY; medical, by 50%+ YoY; storage device, by 10% YoY; auto, flat; 
and sports to decline by 5%. We revised down our 2026-28E EPS by 0.3-
8.5% after factoring in its 3Q26 results.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating with an unchanged 
12M TP of TWD205, now based on a target PER of 17x (from 18x; above 
the 15x average of its past-3-year range of 11-25x) on our 1-year forward 
EPS forecast. We lower the PER target slightly on its decelerating earnings 
momentum. Key downside risks: lower than-expected global auto, medical, 
and AI server component demand; worse-than-expected gross margin.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 18-22% below the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more conservative about PMX’s revenue and 
operating margins.

9 November 2026

3Q26 results review: weaker-than-expected margins

➢ 3Q26 net profit missed our and consensus estimates by 10-11% 
➢ Semiconductor, AI server and Others segments to support growth  
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call with unchanged 12M TP of TWD205

Company profile 
Founded in 1987, Global PMX provides mechanical parts for automotive, medical equipment, and 
storage device components. Its major automotive clients include Continental, Bosch, BorgWarner, ZF, 
Hilite and Tesla. Other major clients are Seagate, Medtronic, and Zimmer.

### Page 5

5

Global PMX (4551 TT): 9 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 8 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and Chairperson of PMX are not the same individual. The board includes 3
independent directors, including 1 female independent director. The Chairman has held this
position at PMX for more than 20 years, indicating the company's strong management capability
and leadership, in our view.
Capital management 1
PMX's payout ratio was 80-90% before 2018, but dropped to 29-69% in 2019-25, which we
attribute to its acquisitions over the past few of years (eg, merger with Sixxon in 2020). We view
these acquisitions as reasonable capital management as PMX’s product line expansion has led
to lower risk exposure due to diversification, helping PMX's 2020-24 revenue to stay relatively
intact.
Related party &
transaction 2 No major revenue from related-party transactions in 2020-25, and we observed no severe issues.
S Product quality & safety 2
PMX holds numerous international product quality certifications, including ISO9002, QS9000 and
ISO/TS16949, which help to regulate the production phases, from development to quality control
and sales. Besides, products made by PMX are 100% compliant with laws/regulations and
market expectations on health and safety, so we believe that product quality and safety concerns
are not a high risk for PMX.
S Materials sourcing & efficiency 2 Global PMX's supply conditions of raw materials (mainly copper and steel) are stable, and its suppliers are required to sign the company’s Supplier Commitment Statement. Therefore, we believe materials sourcing and efficiency will be under control going forward.
S Product design & lifecycle management 2 PMX has adopted strategies that avoid price competition by targeting production of higher value- added products (including auto, medical, semiconductor, and green energy products). Thus, we believe that PMX is continually improving its product lifecycle management.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 3

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
A Closer Look at the Fundamentals
Why we changed our minds – March caution, May upside
surprise
We downgraded Winbond and Nanya Tech in March (Old Memory: Turning Selective) as
elevated DDR4 pricing encouraged supply responses and customer pushback, pointing to
narrower shortages and moderating price hikes. In May, we reversed those calls as lower
expected supply from Micron and Hynix, alongside stronger server and enterprise SSD

demand, widened the projected supply-demand gap (Old Memory: Upside Surprise
Ahead). The distinction matters: March was a selective DDR4 call; today, we see a broader
challenge to old memory’s pricing durability.
What has changed
Pricing power cannot offset weaker demand indefinitely
Our constructive thesis was that niche memory, with limited fixed-price L TA constraints,
could reprice faster and outperform mainstream memory. We now see that flexibility

becoming a two-way risk. Rising uncertainty over cloud spending weakens confidence in
incremental enterprise demand, while much weaker consumer demand challenges
customers’ ability to absorb further hikes.
After the US midterms, our US team now expects ~20% of 2028e US
data center capacity additions to shift to 2029
This include some projects in Texas and Ohio, as governors introduce new data center
legislation (refer to Buy the Drop: AI Investment Opportunities Post the US Midterms by

Stephen Byrd). Stephen has shifted ~5GW of US data center capacity additions from 2028
to 2029  (Exhibit 11 ) – equivalent to 20% of US and 10% of global 2028 capacity
additions. This implies a 4% Y/Y decline in US data center GW additions  in 2028 before a
38% Y/Y rise in 2029. We now expect global data center additions to increase 9% in 2028
and    20% Y/Y  in 2029.
This could create an air pocket across the tech supply chain in 2028,
potentially reducing pricing power and leading to an inventory build

Our latest PC semis work highlights procurement pull-ins in 1Q–3Q26, followed by a
deeper correction in 4Q, reinforcing the risk that earlier orders overstated underlying
consumption (PC Semis: More Divergent Outlooks).
Supply-demand update –  looking beyond the shortage
Our revised models for SLC NAND, MLC NAND, NOR, and DDR4 point to progressively
more balanced markets, particularly into 2028. We now expect 2028 supply-demand
deficits of -3%, -5%, and -1% for SLC NAND, MLC NAND, NOR, with a +1% excess for DDR4.

Near-term tightness need not disappear immediately for stocks to de-rate: a narrowing
shortage can reduce incremental pricing power before earnings peak. We therefore place

Idea
4
less weight on extrapolating recent ASP gains and more on normalized demand and
supply responses and sustainable profitability.

Winbond: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
We adjust our EPS estimates +8% for 2026, -9% for 2027, and -22% for 2028:  The
2026 EPS increase reflects 3Q26 actual results. We also factor in a potential peak in
pricing power across NOR, SLC, and DDR4. Compared to the previous estimate, we now
reduce our flash wafer ASP assumptions throughout 2027-2028 and  26% into 4Q28, and
reduce our DRAM ASP per bit estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  23% into 4Q28,

without changing our flash or DRAM shipment estimates. Apart from reduced revenue
estimates, we also lower our gross margin estimates to account for the reduced ASP
estimates. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of changes in
our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make these
changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and
potentially delays in 2028 US cloud capex.

Winbond: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to  NT$120
We continue to apply P/B multiple methodology to derive our price target, in line with our
approach for Greater China memory IDM peers and in view of the industry's high earnings
volatility. Our price target (base case scenario value) is set at 1.2x 2027e BVPS vs. our
prior target multiple of 2.9x 2027e BVPS, to reflect the potential peak in pricing power.
Our 1.2x target P/B is above our 1.0x P/B valuation anchor –  we still favor the company's

CUBE and S-SiCap businesses for their long-term potential. We see 1.0x P/B as the
downside valuation anchor because book value provides a psychological support level for
capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during cyclical downturns. When earnings are
temporarily depressed, investors typically place greater weight on the underlying net
asset base.
Our base case value/price target cut of 58% is almost entirely a function  of reduced target
multiple, rather than change in 2027e BVPS, which declines only 2% in our new estimate.

Our bull case value is reduced from NT$342 to NT$180, and our bear case value is
reduced from NT$118 to NT$50, based on 1.8x and 0.5x 2027e BVPS, respectively
(previously 3.4x and 1.2x).
We downgrade Winbond to EW
We continue to like the CUBE and S-SiCap businesses for longer-term potential, but we
see near-term pressure from NOR, SLC NAND, and DDR4 businesses, in all of which
industry supply is ramping faster than demand is increasing, especially as global cloud

capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029. We believe the stock still has strong
valuation support at 1.0-1.2x P/B, and therefore we believe an EW rating is fair.

GigaDevice: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
We cut our EPS estimates  25% for 2026, 36% for 2027, and 33% for 2028:  The 2026e
EPS change mainly reflects actual 2Q26 and 3Q26 results. The 2027e and 2028e EPS
changes reflect a potential peak in pricing power across DDR4, NOR Flash, and SLC NAND.
We now reduce our DRAM and others revenue estimates throughout 2027-2028 and 43%
into 4Q28, mainly reflecting changes in our DDR4 ASP estimates. We also reduce our

Flash (including NOR and SLC NAND) ASP estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  25% into
4Q28, without changing our Flash shipment estimates. We did not materially change our
GM assumptions. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of
changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make
these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and
potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

GigaDevice: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target  to Rmb259
We continue to derive our price target, which is our base case value, from a residual
income model. We believe this best captures the stock’s long-term value.
Our base case value cut of 65% can be attributed ~33% to earnings estimate revisions, as
has been elaborated, and ~33% from multiple compression, driven by intermediate growth
rate cut and beta increase.  (Note: the overall price target change is calculated from the

fully revised RI model. The individual contribution estimates are illustrative and should
not be added together or compounded to derive the overall change.).
We cut our intermediate growth rate to 8.0% from 11.3% to reflect stronger changes in
the supply environment compared to the end market demand over the medium to long
term. The potential peak in pricing power for niche memory (DDR4, SLC, MLC, NOR) and
the potential delay of 2028 US cloud capex now suggest slower revenue growth for

GigaDevice from 2026 to 2036. We think a 3.3ppt p.a. growth rate cut reflects the new
supply/demand dynamics with a closer gap.
We also raise our beta to 1.50 from 1.14, leading our cost of equity assumption to rise to
11.8% from 9.4%. This  reflects a cautious valuation scenario, to account for the recent
memory segment volatility, 70%+ correction from the stock price peak in June, and the
fabless model, which does not provide a floor support for valuation similar to the IDM

model. The elevated beta reflects the company's exposure to rapidly changing niche
memory pricing dynamics that could severely affect earnings with a downward bias
compared to our financial forecasts.
Our other key assumptions remain unchanged, including equity risk premium of 6.5%, risk-
free rate of 2% and payout ratio of 40%.
Our bull case value falls to Rmb448 from Rmb1,300, and our bear case value to Rmb112
from Rmb324.
We downgrade GigaDevice to EW

We still see the company as a domestic leader in MCU localization and believe its
customized DRAM business could show a revenue ramp over the next 1-2 years. We also
still like its strategic partnership with CXMT, which provides both capacity support and
process tech improvements. However, we see near-term pressure from the NOR, SLC
NAND, and DDR4 businesses, in all of which industry supply is ramping faster than
demand increase, especially as global cloud capex may face partial delay from 2028 to

2029. Fabless design houses do not face depreciation and utilization issues during a down-
cycle, but at the same time they lack a similar valuation anchor as IDM/foundry players
from their book value, and therefore we see potentially continued high volatility ahead.
Our new price target implies a 2027e P/E of 9x, below the historical average of 38x since
2016, owing to changes in pricing power.

PSMC: Earnings Estimate     Revisions
We    adjust our EPS forecasts +10% for 2026 and -11% each for 2027 and 2028:      The
2026e EPS change mainly reflects actual 2Q26 and 3Q26 results. The 2027e and 2028e
EPS changes reflect a potential peak in pricing power across DDR3/3 and SLC NAND. We
now reduce our DRAM wafer ASP estimates throughout 2027-2028 and 19% into 4Q28,
without changing our DRAM wafer shipment estimates. We did not materially change our

GM assumptions. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of
changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make
these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and
potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

PSMC: Valuation     Methodology
Our price target  is cut to NT$54
We incorporate our revised estimates and maintain our P/B valuation methodology. Our
new target, which is our base case value,  is based on a 2027e P/B of 1.0x, down from 2.0x
previously –  below the historical average of 1.5x since 2021, to reflect pricing
uncertainties.
Our base case value/price target cut of 51% is almost entirely a result of target multiple
reduction, rather than change in 2027e BVPS, which declined only 1.5% in our new

estimate. For PSMC, we use 1.0x P/B as the downside valuation anchor –  book value
provides a psychological support level for capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during
cyclical downturns. When earnings are temporarily depressed, investors typically place
greater weight on the underlying net asset base.
We decrease our bull case value from NT$177 to NT$86 and our bear case value from NT
$55 to NT$27, implying 1.6x and 0.5x 2027e P/B, respectively.

We downgrade PSMC to EW
We are positive on the company's emerging 3D AI foundry business, and we believe PSMC
is well positioned to benefit from its Micron partnership, including front-end migration to
the one-year  process node and back-end opportunities in advanced DRAM production
(e.g., HBM). The company is also catching up with the DRAM pricing up-cycle.
At the same time, its logic foundry business is set to enjoy continued price hikes into 2027.

However, on its memory foundry side, we see near-term pressure from DDR3/4, in which
industry supply is ramping faster than demand is increasing, especially as global cloud
capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029.
We believe the stock still has strong valuation support at 1.0x P/B, and therefore we
believe an EW rating is fair.

Macronix: Earnings  Estimate Revisions
We cut our 2026, 2027, and 2028 EPS forecasts 14%, 20%, and 21%, respectively: The
2026e EPS change mainly reflects actual 3Q26 results. The 2027e and 2028e EPS
changes reflect a potential peak in pricing power across NOR and MLC/SLC NAND. We
now reduce our NAND and /NOR wafer ASP estimates throughout 2027-2028 and 15%
and 8% into 4Q28 respectively, without changing our wafer shipment estimates. We did

not materially change our GM assumptions. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are
largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a
good time to make these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating
price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

Macronix: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$90 from NT$220
We continue to use a P/B multiple methodology to derive our price target, which is our
base case value, in line with our approach for Greater China memory IDM peers and in
view of the industry's high volatility. Our new target is based on a 2027e P/B of 1.0x, down
from 2.1x previously – below the average of 1.5x since 2017, given the potential peak in
pricing power for NOR and SLC/MLC NAND. For Macronix, we now use 1.0x P/B as the

downside valuation anchor because book value provides a psychological support level for
capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during cyclical downturns. When earnings are
temporarily depressed, investors typically place greater weight on the underlying net
asset base.
Our base case value/price target cut of 59% reflects both target multiple reduction and a
15% 2027 BVPS estimate reduction (relatively large because of a smaller base).
We cut our bull case value from NT$355 to NT$145, which is 1.6x our 2027e BVPS. We cut

our bear case value from NT$100 to NT$41, which is 0.5x our 2027e BVPS.
We downgrade Macronix to EW
We continue to like its unique position in MLC NAND as the only major supplier still
ramping up capacity. However, we do see near-term pressure from NOR, DDR3/4, and
even SLC/MLC NAND, in all of which  industry supply is ramping faster than demand is
increasing, especially as global cloud capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029. We
believe the stock still has strong valuation support at 1.0x P/B, and therefore we believe

an EW rating is fair.

Nanya Tech: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
Our 2026 estimates are unchanged;  we cut our 2027 and 2028 EPS estimates  7% and
9%,respectively: We factor in a potential peak in DDR3/4 pricing power, which affects
Nanya Tech's top line and margins. We now reduce our Nanya Tech DRAM ASP per bit
estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  6% into 4Q28, without changing our wafer
shipment estimates. We did not materially change our GM assumptions. Our changes in

net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross
profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make these changes now as a result of the
narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

Morgan Stanley Research 35
Nanya Tech: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target from NT$600 to NT$550
Our price target (base case scenario value) reflects a target BVPS multiple of 2.04x for
2027 (was 4.47x) versus ROE of 59% for 2027.  We lower our target multiple to reflect
potential peak in DDR4 pricing power and recent memory segment volatility. For Nanya
Tech, our new base case target P/B multiple of 2.04x P/B is stronger than our downside

valuation anchor of 1.0x P/B for capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks.  This is because we
expect Nanya to remain relatively resilient compared to other DDR4 players in our
coverage, given stronger process technology and potential DDR5 migration.
Our base case value/price target cut of 8% reflects both target multiple reduction and
BVPS estimate adjustment of 3% arising from earnings estimate revisions.
We cut our bull and bear case values from NT$1,270 and NT$330 to NT$1,150 and NT

$300, respectively -- 4.27x and 1.12x 2027e BVPS.
We remain OW
Despite potentially peaking pricing power seen in DDR4, we remain positive on DDR5 in
view of continued strong demand from servers. Nanya is already producing DDR5 on its
second-generation 10-nm process, with DDR5 contributing ~10% of revenue; its next
process enters customer qualification in 1Q27, and EUV implementation is targeted for
2028. This establishes its edge in process technology vs the other DDR4 vendors in our

coverage and a stronger position in DDR5 migration. Valuation is also attractive, in our
view. Therefore, we remain OW on Nanya Tech.

AP Memory: Estimate revisions summary
We  adjust our EPS estimates  +21% for 2026, +2% for 2027, and -12% for 2028: We
factor in 3Q26 preliminary results. We adjust our 2028 forecasts  mainly to account for
supply chain risks. We note that the company's IoTRAM is less affected by  volatility of
typical commodity DRAM pricing. The 2027e and 2028e EPS changes mainly reflect
supply chain uncertainties around S-SiCap. We now reduce our S-SiCap unit shipment

estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  50%+ into 4Q28. We also slightly reduced  our IoT
RAM ASP estimates throughout 2027-28 to reflect peaking DDR3/4 pricing power. In
addition, we reduced our GM assumptions across product lines to reflect potential  peak in
pricing power and supply chain uncertainties. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates
are largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is
a good time to make these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating

price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

(59.7% in 3Q, down from 65.3% in 2Q). In 4Q26 and 2027, we expect emerging weakness

Phison: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target from NT$2,588 to NT$1,357
Our price target is our base case value, derived from a residual income (RI) model. Our cut
reflects our latest earnings estimates. We also cut our terminal growth rate assumption
from 4.0% to 3.0% to be broadly in line with a conservative estimate of long-term
nominal economic growth in developed economies. We believe this is appropriate for

Phison because rising storage demand should support continued growth, while declining
NAND prices per bit, competition, and maturing SSD penetration should limit the
conversion of volume growth into earnings growth, bringing its growth closer to that of
the broader economy over time.
Our other RI parameters are unchanged, including a cost of equity of 9.0%, a medium-
term growth rate of 9.0%, and a dividend payout ratio assumption of 50.0%.
Apart from the earnings estimate revisions and terminal growth rate change, a material

part of the price target change is driven by an error in the previously published residual
income model (August 2026). The error has now been corrected. In the previous RI model,
the 2028e net income was mistakenly calculated as 2027e net income * (1+ intermediate
growth rate), where it should have been linked to the actual 2028e net income forecast.
This overstated the previous price target.
Our earnings estimates have also changed materially  from the previously published

version: they increase 12% for 2026, 60% for 2027, and 37% for  2028. This mainly reflects
higher GM assumptions of 61.2%, 51.9%, and 42.0%, respectively; previously we assumed
60.1%, 43.4%, and 39.5%.
Our base case value/price target is lowered 48%, of which roughly +37% is contributed by
earnings estimate revisions, -6% is contributed by terminal growth rate reduction, and -
83% is contributed by the correction of the prior RI model error. (The overall price target

change is calculated from the fully revised RI model. The individual contribution estimates
are illustrative and should not be added together or compounded to derive the overall
change.)
We cut our bull and bear case values from NT$3,400 and NT$1,250 to NT$1,782 and NT
$655, respectively, implying 8.4x 2027e EPS in the bull case and 3.1x 2027e EPS in the
bear case.
We downgrade Phison to UW
The company hopes its AiDaptive module and service business can pick up and offset the

margin pressure in the conventional module business in the coming quarters, which we
believe is worth monitoring. However, its 3Q GM  of 59% missed the Street estimate of
60.3%; we see this as a potential sign that the previously strong margin profile supported
by low-cost inventory may start to face more downside. Specifically, the AI storage part of
its business (eSSD, and cSSD for AI PC, together 40% of revenue) may face margin
decline. This is what we have been cautious about, and we see the 3Q margin miss as

having provided a good opportunity to downgrade this stock, which we view as

overvalued based on our revised assumptions.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 177: 261111_gs_avc

Source PDF: all report pdf/261111_gs_avc.pdf

### Page 2

Earnings revision: 
We revise up our 2026 earnings estimates by 6% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise 
down our 2026E revenue estimates by 1%). We revise up 2028E earnings estimates by 
1% to reflect a better product mix with higher contribution from the server business. We 
now expect the GM expansion to be faster due to the higher server business 
contribution, and thus revise up GPM by 1.2/0.3/0.4ppt in 2026/27/28E.

Valuation: 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AVC with a new 12m TP of NT$4,820 (from NT$4,736), 
based on 30.6x 2027E P/E (which is above AVC’s historical average +1 STDV. forward 
trading P/E, reflecting our positive view on AVC on the rising penetration rate of 
liquid-cooled AI servers), and factor in our latest earnings revision. 
Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

AVC is a global leading supplier of thermal solutions on Server, PC, Telecom and 
consumer electronics. We expect the company’s cooling revenue contributions to grow 
to 66%/71%/74% in 2026/27/28E, driven by: (1) rising computing power of AI servers 
driving liquid cooling demand, (2) capacity ramp-up in Vietnam of 3D Vapor Chambers 
for GPUs and liquid cooling components, and (3) AI PC’s launch in 2025, featuring more 
powerful CPU/ NPU/ Memory. We are Buy rated. Our target price is based on 2027E P/E 
of 30.6x which is higher than its past 5-yr avg.+1stv. of 11x, reflecting the product mix 
upgrade toward servers cooling / liquid cooling with stronger earnings growth and 
better margins, which we believe supports the higher valuation. 
We are Buy rated on AVC with a 12m TP of NT$4,820, based on 30.6 2027E P/E, which is

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

SK하이닉스 (000660)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상  
3Q26 영업이익: 상반기와 다르다. 억제 요인들의 추세 반전 전환 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 전분기 대비 32% 증가한 80.1조원으로 시장기대
치를 능가할 전망이다. 최근 원화강세 흐름 및 상반기 동사의 상대적 실적개선 속도 
열위에 3Q26 영업이익 추정치는 70조원까지 내려갔다 (컨센서스 74조원). 하지만 
이제 동사 출하량 및 판가의 탄력적 복원세가 경쟁사 보다 빠른 실적 개선을 이끌 전
망이다. 3Q26 DRAM과 NAND 출하량/판가 증감률은 각각 +13%/+22%, 
+4%/+15% 수준으로, 각 영업이익은 63조원/17조원으로 예상된다 (OPM 각각 
84%/69% 추정). 그간의 상대적 실적 열위는 이제 반전된다. 이는 1) 판가 전략에 
있어 중장기적 관점에서 고객 중심 유연성 극대화를 꾀했던 부분의 보상적 되돌림이 
발생하고, 2) 업계에서 제일 높은 HBM 판매 비중으로 인해 판가 상승률에 발목 잡혔
지만, 오히려 HBM4 본격 공급이 시작되며 이익기여가 본격화된다. 아울러 3) 모바
일 핵심고객과의 기성 계약이 산업 전반적 LTA 논의로 재검토되며 공급사에 크게 유
리한 조건으로 변화되어 가기 시작한다. 당사는 1Q27부터 모바일 메모리 판가 급등
을 전망하며 (그림1), 2026E/2027E 영업이익의 경우 264조원/454조원으로 상향조정한
다. 4Q26 내 시장 실적 추정치 상향조정 및 투자자 수급의 구조적 개선을 전망한다.

‘지능기술’ 시대, 수요의 가격 역탄력성 속 중장기 기회창출에 주목 
과거 B2C 사이클과 다르게, 금번 B2B 사이클은 수요의 가격 역탄력성을 보이고 있
다. AGI 도달을 위한 선착순 투자가 핵심이기 때문이다. 빅테크 중 낙오자가 발생하
기 전까지 경쟁적 대규모 투자는 지속된다. 최근 동사의 솔리다임 상장 추진보도에 
일부 우려가 제기되고 있지만, 투자자들은 그 이면의 중장기적 기회에 주목해야 한다. 
동사는 LTA 외에도, 다양한 파트너십을 추진하며 중장기 시장 예측력을 증가시키고 
있다. 솔리다임 상장 과정에서 유입되는 현금은 모회사 ‘SK AI co’의 지분교환 및 JV 
구축 등에 활용되리라 예상된다. 이는 중장기 수요를 ‘창출’시키는 개념이다.

순수한 주주가치 제고 목적 환원 재도출 예상. 적정주가 상향조정 
빠르게 증가한 현금흐름 중 ‘잉여’분은 주주가치 ‘회복’에 우선 배분돼야 함이 마땅하
며, 동사 역시 최근 공시를 통해 주주가치 제고와 주가의 저평가 인식을 드러냈다. 현
재 진행중인 40조원 자사주 매입 프로그램 종료 후 즉각적인 신규 환원 정책 도출이 
예상된다. 2027년 메모리 공급부족은 명확하다. 역사적으로 효율적 시장 내, 주가 조
정은 탄력적 복원으로 귀결되어왔다. 동사에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하며, 적정주
가의 경우 320만원으로 상향한다 (26E/27E 평균 P/B 4.0배 적용). 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 12

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 3,200,000원 
현재주가 (10.8) 1,681,000원 
상승여력 90.4%

## Document 241: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡_62bfa2bd

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡_62bfa2bd.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

Company Brief 
유틸리티/건설 
Analyst 문경원, CFA 
kyeongwon.moon@meritz.co.kr 
RA 이동석 
dongseok.lee@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 Review: 바닥을 잡는다면 지금  
믹스 개선에 따른 이익률 개선 조금씩 관찰 중 
3Q26 연결 영업이익은 2,612억원(+90.6% YoY) 을 기록하며 컨센서스(2,455억원)
를 소폭 상회했다. 밥캣(1,674억원)이 컨센서스(1,761억원)를 소폭 하회한 가운데, 
에너빌리티 부문 영업이익(+179% YoY, OPM 6.6%) 이 호실적을 이끌었다. 환율 하
락에도 불구, 가스터빈, 원전 등 고수익성 프로젝트의 비중 상승이 주요 원인이다. 
3Q26 누적 수주는 10.0조원(+85.5% YoY) 을 기록했다. 4Q26 가스복합 EPC 수주 
등을 감안하면 연간 가이던스(13.3조원) 달성은 무난하다. 한수원과의 협상 결렬로 
이목이 집중된 체코 두코바니 시공 수주는 2027년 인식될 확률이 높아 보인다. 주간
사가 바뀌더라도 이미 프로젝트에 참여 중인 동사를 배제하기는 쉽지 않다.

대미투자 이후, 연말~연초 수주 모멘텀에 집중 
연말 연초에 다음과 수주 모멘텀이 집중되면서, 주가 상승을 이끌 전망이다. 
1) 대형원전: 정부 자금 중심의 Westinghouse LLI(장납기 기자재) 발주가 몰린다. 
우선 미국 DOE(에너지부)가 제공한 대출을 기반으로 미국 수주가 기대된다.  폴란드 
정부 역시 최근 LLI 발주 작업을 시작했다. 한편, 한국 정부는 연말까지 100억 달러 
규모의 대미 투자 송금을 협의 중인데, 이 역시 DOC(상무부) 주도의 대형 원전 건설
을 위한 LLI 발주에 쓰일 가능성이 높다 
2) SMR: 오래 기다려왔던 Nuscale 향 수주가 시작되고 있다. 우선 29일 모듈 1기 
제작 계약을 체결했다. 현금이 부족한 Nuscale이 용기를 냈다는 건, 곧 Nuscale-
TVA 간 PPA 가 임박했음을 의미한다. 본격적인 수주는 PPA 직후 예상된다.  
3) 가스 터빈: 2026년 초 해외 수주(7기)는 2029년까지 인도 계획이다. 이후 2030
년 슬랏 예약을 완료하고 2031년 물량을 논의 중으로 보인다. 지금은 2030년 슬랏
에 대한 PO가 나와 수주잔고에 인식할 타이밍이다. 역시 수출 물량으로 추측한다.

성장에 대한 기대가 사라진 밸류에이션, 지금이 바닥 
투자의견 Buy를 유지, 할인율 상승을 반영하여 적정주가를 109,000원으로 -17% 
하향 조정한다. 금리 상승, 환율 하락, 트럼프 지지율 하락 등 Top-down 리스크에 
최근 조정을 받았지만, 추가적인 주가 하락 폭은 제한적일 전망이다. EV/선행 수주잔
고 지표는 현재 1.0배 수준까지 하락하며, 원전의 성장성이 주목받지 못했던 
2021~2024년 수준에 근접하게 내려왔다.  
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 30

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 109,000원 
현재주가 (10.29) 56,700원 
상승여력 92.2%

## Document 333: daily story_UAA Under Armour_劉昃恩_20261109

Source PDF: all report pdf/daily story_UAA Under Armour_劉昃恩_20261109.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  運動用品  ‧美國

Under Armour 
(UAA.O/UAA US)

客流量下滑考驗 
重要訊息  
Under Armour  2Q FY27 (7-9 月；財年止於 3 月) 營運約當 損平。儘管 FY27
營收年減中個位數之 展望不變，管理層下調毛利率指引，反映考量油價、
運價和庫存清倉等衝擊迎面而來 。 
評論及分析  
2Q FY27 營運保持損平 。2Q FY27 每股盈餘 0.00 美元，符合預期。營收
12.4 億美元，年減幅度擴大至 7%，反映全產品 /通路需求 皆轉差。地區別
方面，北美、歐非中東及亞太平洋銷售分別年減 7%、9%、9%，僅拉美
為唯一成長市場 ，年增 3%。再加上通 路、地區組合不利與折扣力道加大，
毛利率延續衰退走勢， 年減 0.3、季減 7.1 個百分點至 47.0%，拖累營利率
至僅 1.2%，年減 0.1、季減 3.1 個百分點。  
產業困境 。管理層觀察到歐美客流量下滑與同業促銷力道加大風險，預計
3Q FY27 營收將年減 8-9% (連三季惡化 )，劣於市場共識之 4%衰退；其中，
北美及歐非中東將為 風暴中心， 分別年減低雙位數、高個位數，亞太則 將
改善至約年持平。加上油價和運價上漲、庫存清倉與匯率不利 等因素疊加
影響，FY27 毛利率指引下調至 47.2-47.2% (前次為 47.7-48.2%)，且年增
1.7-2.2 個百分點中約有 1.5 個百分點係來自美國退稅挹注 。 
庫存去化策略 。2Q FY27 庫存週轉天數年增 7%、但季減 17%至 149 天，
仍高於過去十年同期平均水準之 131 天，主因營運規模縮減凸顯在途存貨
較高。管理層表示 正與合作工廠協商推遲部分發貨，同時考慮 2H FY27 從
批發端去化庫存，但將避開同業清倉甩賣的場所。 公司計畫持續減少產品
SKU、聚焦高端或細分領域 (例如美式足球釘鞋已成為美國第二大品牌 )，
同時簡化工作流程 ，確保長期利潤率改善空間 。 
投資建議  
考量歐美客流量逆風 與促銷影響 仍延續，我們維持 Under Armour 「持有」
評等，目標價改採 FY28 每股淨值 1.6 倍推算，調整為 5.5 美元。 
投資風險  
上檔風險為消費較預期強勁與品牌 重拾人氣。

### Page 2

美國 Under Armour

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

Under Armour 11 月 6 日法說會重點摘要  
 2Q FY27 (財年止於 3 月；7-9 月) 每股盈餘為 0.00 美元，符合預期。
營收 12.4 億美元，年減幅度擴大至 7%，反映全產品及全通路需求皆
轉差。受通路、地區組合不利與 折扣力道加大 影響，毛利率年減 0.3、
季減 7.1 個百分點至 47.0%，拖累營利率至僅 1.2%。 
 產品別營收：服飾  (佔總營收 71%)、鞋類 (19%) 及配件  (8%) 全面
惡化至年減 6%、11%、7%。 
 市場別營收：北美 (佔總營收 59%)、歐非中東  (23%) 及亞太平洋  
(13%) 分別年減 7%、9%、9%，符合公司指引。拉美  (4%) 年增
3%，為唯一成長市場。 
 通路別營收：批發業務  (佔總營收 58%) 年減 6%，再度轉弱，主因
全價商品銷售疲軟及部分分銷管道遇 逆風。直接面對消費者  (DTC，
40%) 衰退擴大至 8%，反映客流量壓力導致 直營店與電商業務分別
年減 7%、12%。 
 2Q FY27 庫存週轉天數年增 7%、但季減 17%至 149 天，仍高於過去
十年同期平均水準之 131 天，主因在途存貨較高 。公司正透過與合作
工廠協商推遲部分發貨， 同時考慮 2H FY27 從批發端去化庫存 ，但將
避開同業清倉甩賣 的場所。  
 FY27 營收維持年減中個位數之 展望，但基於油價和運價上漲、庫存
清倉與匯率不利等因素影響， 將毛利率指引下調至 47.2-47.2% (前次
為 47.7-48.2%)，且年增 1.7-2.2 個百分點中 約有 1.5 個百分點係 來自
美國退稅挹注。加上組織轉型與重組費用認列，預計每股虧損 0.02
至每股盈餘 0.02 美元。 
 短期而言，管理層觀察到歐美客流量下滑與同業促銷力道加大風險，
預計 3Q FY27 營收將年減 8-9%；其中，北美及歐非中東將分別年減
低雙位數、高個位數，亞太約呈年持平。 加上毛利率年增 0.3-0.5 個
百分點展望， 3Q FY27 營運落後市場預期。

圖 6: 公司概況  圖 7: 服飾貢獻營收最大比重 
成立於 1996 年， Under Armour 為國際領導運動品牌，致力
提供專業運動服飾、鞋類及配件；其產品採用創新材料技術，
包括吸濕排汗、溫度控管、緩衝與布料永續利用等，目的在
創造差異化，並提升消費者品牌 體驗。美洲為 FY26 營收主要
來源地區，佔比達 58%，其次分別為歐非中東 24%、亞太平
洋 14%、拉丁美洲 4%。

股價說明  等級 定義 
 增加持股  (OP) 對個股持正面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現超越凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的 總
報酬。  
 持有  (N) 對個股持中性看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現符合凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總
報酬。 
 降低持股  (U) 對個股持負面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現低於凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總
報酬。 
 未評等 (NR) 凱基證券未對該個股加以評等。  
 受法規限制  
未評等 (R) 
受凱基證券集團內部政策和 /或相關法令限制使凱基證券集團無法進行某些形式的資訊交
流，其中包括提供評等給投資人參考。  
  *總報酬 = (十二個月目標價 -現價)/現價 
免責聲明

## Document 344: Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December

Source PDF: all report pdf/Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December
Date:    7 hours ago
Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In
December
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 10 Nov 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by liam ward / Unsplash
I. The Fed Has More Work To Do
On October 26, we warned that the Fed could surprise markets with a
25bps rate hike at its October meeting. If the Fed chose to pass on doing
so because the midterm elections were too close, we predicted a 50bps
hike in December. Two days later, the FOMC voted 10-2 to hold the
federal funds rate (FFR) at 3.75%-4.00%, with the two dissenters favoring
a 25bps hike. Yet, in his October 28 presser, Fed Chair Kevin Warsh's

### Page 3

assessment of the economy sounded decidedly hawkish. Economic
growth was robust and broadening, the labor market was in good shape,
inflation remained above the Fed's 2% target, and financial conditions
were hardly tight.
So why did the Fed stand pat?
Warsh argued that financial conditions had tightened, inflation
expectations remained anchored, and stronger productivity growth was
allowing the economy to expand without overheating. With an information-

rich period ahead, the Fed saw little reason to rush. We called that
decision puzzling, comparing it to someone standing in a room with a fire
alarm blaring and smoke pouring under the door, yet insisting on more
evidence before calling the fire department.
Now Fed Governor Christopher Waller is sounding the same alarm.
Speaking in London on Tuesday, Waller said he would support a 25bps
rate hike in December even if inflation improved in October. However, if

October's core CPI increased by around 0.3% m/m or more, as it had in
the previous two months, he argued that a larger move would be
warranted. Specifically, he said that "a 50 basis point hike in the policy
rate should be considered at the December meeting." Waller warned that
moving by only 25bps under those circumstances would jeopardize the
Fed's inflation-fighting credibility.
Economic data released since Waller's remarks suggest that a 50bps hike

in December is becoming increasingly likely. We believe such a move is
warranted, as it would bring the FFR back to our estimated neutral range
of 4.25%-4.50%. Consider the following:
(1) CPI Inflation. October's headline CPI rose by 0.4% m/m, while core
CPI increased 0.3% m/m for the third consecutive month (chart).
Inflationary pressures remain broad-based, with particularly strong
increases in energy, food, and transportation services. Importantly, the

core CPI reading meets Waller's condition for necessitating a 50bps rate
hike in December.

### Page 4

Three forces are contributing to renewed goods inflation: the AI
investment boom, higher energy prices stemming from the Iran war, and
tariffs. In October, prices rose sharply in tariff-sensitive categories such as
furniture & bedding and appliances (chart). However, overall durable
goods prices were essentially unchanged, largely reflecting a 1.2%
decline in used car and truck prices.
Inflationary pressures were also widespread among nondurable goods,

with prices rising 0.5% m/m in October (chart). Recreational reading
materials, housekeeping supplies, footwear, and energy saw particularly
sharp increases. Apparel prices were essentially unchanged, while prices
for computer and software accessories fell.

### Page 5

Another concerning aspect in the inflation data is the persistence of
services inflation, reflecting resilient consumer demand, housing cost
pressures, and the pass-through of higher energy prices. Services prices
rose 0.4% m/m in October, including a 0.4% increase in services
excluding shelter (chart). Postage and delivery, hospital services,
transportation services, and motor vehicle insurance rose sharply.
Owners' equivalent rent also rose 0.3%.

(2) Small Business Sentiment. Echoing the CPI report's fire alarm,
October's NFIB survey showed that the net share of small business
owners planning to raise selling prices jumped to 37%, the highest since
July 2022, while 24% planned to raise worker compensation, the highest
since December 2025 (chart).

### Page 6

(3) Consumer spending. Spending remains remarkably robust. Redbook
same-store retail sales rose 10.1% y/y in the week of November 6, well
above the 2025 full-year average of 5.8% (chart).
(4) Labor market. One of the key reasons consumer spending remains
remarkably strong is the continued strength of the labor market. The latest
ADP data support this assessment. Private employers added an average
of 19,750 jobs per week during the four weeks ending October 24 (chart).

That translates into roughly 86,000 jobs per month, a healthy pace of job
creation that is sufficient to keep the unemployment rate near its current
low level of 4.1%.

### Page 7

clause.
Yardeni QuickTakes © 2026 – Unsubscribe

## Document 386: New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-13)

Source PDF: all report pdf/New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-13).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• As polls suggested, the election was a nailbiter
that has not yet been resolved. Election night
results show a lead to the centre-right on
paper in a significantly enlarged parliament.
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First
and ACT won 67 seats. The centre-left bloc
of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti Māori won 57
seats. There was a swing away from National
to its coalition partners while the centre left
grouping picked up a few seats.

• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling
and will enter parliament with at least 7 seats,
reducing what could have been a larger swing
to the centre-left grouping.
• The size of parliament will expand by around 9
seats as National held onto a large share of its
electorate votes, entitling it to more seats than
implied by its party vote share. As a result,
a bloc would require 66 seats to command a
majority in parliament.
• Several senior National ministers have not

been re-elected, including Finance Minister
Nicola Willis. She will continue in a caretaker
role but will need to be replaced in any new
centre-right government.
• Significant uncertainty about the result exists.
National might lose perhaps two marginal
electorates in the final analysis. There are
400,000 special votes to count which may
shift the list seat allocations as these tend
to reduce the share of the centre-right bloc
by 1.6-1.7ppts. Hence, when all is said and

done, the centre-right’s 10 seat advantage on
election night could reduce by 1-4 seats.
• Should this play out the centre-right would not
have sufficient seats to reach a majority in an
enlarged house and would require the support
of the Opportunity Party to govern.
• The final election outcome will not be
known until 27 November. Serious coalition
negotiations likely won’t begin until then
and delays could occur. Should recounts be
required, some further delay is possible.

• Based on these results, we see a higher
probability of a centre-right government
ultimately emerging that likely will require
the support of the Opportunity Party in some
form. The Opportunity Party likely won’t enter
Cabinet but may offer support on confidence
and supply in exchange for policy concessions.
• But a centre-left government can’t be ruled
out if special votes heavily favour the centre
left bloc, and especially if an agreement
between the Opportunity Party and the centre-

right parties can’t be reached.
• Regardless of the final count, the results
suggest a government with weak support and
ongoing vulnerabilities. Hence the prospect of
controversial large changes may be modest.
For a centre-right government that might
limit the extent of fiscal consolidation, while
the centre left’s plans for larger scale tax and
spending initiatives could be trimmed back.
• A centre-right government won’t change the
RBNZ mandate while a centre-left government

likely still will. A new finance minister will be
required with the possibility of ACT leader
David Seymour taking on that role.
